# Skills · L · part 21

818 entries, learning-game-design to least-squares-optimization-spectral-deconvolution. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **learning-game-design**: Designs a learning game so that the play is the practice, before any code is written. Use when someone has an idea for an educational game, asks how to make a subject fun, wants a game design document or a core loop for a learning game, is unsure which genre or mechanic fits a skill, or has a game that is fun but teaches little, or teaches but is dull. ([mext-ai/elearning-agent-skills](https://github.com/mext-ai/elearning-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/learning-game-design))
- **learning-game-feel**: Give a learning interaction readable feedback and game feel without slowing it down or hiding the concept. Use when an interaction works but feels flat (按下去沒反應, 答對答錯長得一樣, 很乾, 沒回饋感), when adding answer feedback, animations, sound, XP/解鎖 effects, or transitions to a teaching site, when a feedback animation is too long or blocks the next action, or when a reward effect needs to stay readable on a mid-range phone, with reduced motion, or with sound off. ([travisebill/minimax-plugins](https://github.com/travisebill/minimax-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/learning-game-studio/skills/learning-game-feel) · 2 copies)
